# Board archive

History moved off `docs/board.md` (Methods 5, "Usage goes further"). Newest archive first; entries as they were on the board.

## Archived 2026-11-23 07:34 (Gold Box Lead): P101 answered (Jeremy: yes); the Manual exception request done

- 2026-11-23 07:00 · coach (Jeremy's restart read) → player, Lead · **The reload rule's Manual of Bodily Health exception (Jeremy decided 2026-11-23 06:57; the standing Reload rule above updated)**: one death in a won fight may be raised instead of reloaded while the Manual is unread and Raise Dead's 5,500 is in hand; the raised member reads the Manual (a day, then 30 days' training: CON +1 back). Why it is cheap: Raise Dead takes 1 CON, and above 14 the hit points of the old bonus; at CON 18 -> 17 a cleric or magic-user loses no HP (the cut is skipped at 17+ for non-fighters, party_member.gd raised_from_dead), a fighter about 1 HP a level (bonus +4 -> +3); the Manual restores the CON but not those HP. Reading it at CON 18 gains nothing (19 gives the same HP bonus). So: a cleric or magic-user always; a fighter only when the replay would be long; two deaths, or after the Manual is read, still a reload. (1) **player:** plans.py `reload_rule` and the README's rule. (2) **Lead:** the playbook's "Win, lose or reload" section (Inbox line 07:00) and ai-business-ideas `ideas/game-modernizer.md` (a dated decision line; this chat couldn't reach that repo). · **Player 48 (07:30): (1) done** (`plans.manual_exception`: a member with no fighter class always, a fighter with `OP_RAISE_FIGHTER=1`; `manualread` / `OP_MANUAL_READ=1` ends it; README "Player 48") · **Lead (2026-11-23 07:24): (2) done** (playbook "Win, lose or reload"; game-modernizer.md Decisions) · answered
- 2026-11-22 17:36 · combat (Combat Chat 47) → design (for Jeremy) · **P101: fallen monsters on the field.** The original draws a downed monster as the skull and crossbones only while GOES DOWN shows, then its squares are bare floor (DOSBox, 2:20's trolls: a troll dying at 36 HP lies unseen until it rises); party members lie as bodies. Ours does the same, so a fallen troll can't be found to stand on. Options: (a) as the original; (b) a QoL addition, FALLEN MONSTERS in SETTINGS (off by default): a monster that is down but not dead (a troll waiting to rise) is drawn as a dim mark on its squares. Recommend (b), off by default: the original's rule stays, and a player who knows trolls can choose to see them. · open

**The reload rule (Jeremy, 2026-11-20 00:40; bot and Player alike):** reload on a wipe, a member dead, a level drained, permanent ability loss, a key item lost, or petrification, unless a temple cures it at a price the party can pay and reach; and on anything that would cost a human player about an hour without an obvious gain. **The Player redoes the original's full run from before the graveyard (4:10) with the rule**, then retries the Stojanow wall, the kobold king and the finale without shortcuts. **Main's bot gets the same rule.** **Combat: four questions from the code and DOSBox:** (1) does Protection from Evil stop level drain; (2) does QUICK (and the Player's tactical mode) use TURN UNDEAD; (3) which temple services Pool sells (Stone to Flesh? Restoration?) and (4) their prices. Answers into docs/playbook.md. **From a GOG forum thread (Jeremy, 00:55; to be confirmed):** temples don't cure drain; Restoration scrolls (library, the clerk's graveyard commission) are the only cure: keep them, use one at once; draining a character twice before restoring breaks it (a bug). Reload rule: drained with a scroll in the packs = use it and go on; without = reload. **Combat Chat 32's answers (read in GAME.OVR; lines in docs/playbook.md "Added by Combat Chat 32"):** (1) **No:** a wight's or spectre's hit drains with no save and no check of the target (0x10FFF / 0x1101D -> 0x10D7D); Protection from Evil's -2 to hit is overwritten before the roll (seen, Combat Chat 10), only its +2 on saves counts. (2) **Yes, QUICK turns by itself:** a QUICK cleric (or paladin 3+) turns once a fight, at its first turn with an unturned undead in sight, after its item weighing and before any spell (0xADC3 -> 0x13B4C, 0x13965; seen at the graveyard, Combat Chat 5); the tactical mode has TURN on the fight menu (the Player's tool). Turning spectres is near hopeless: impossible below cleric level 5, then 20 / 16 / 13 / 10 needed at levels 5-8 (wights 20 / 19 / 16 / 10 / 7 / 4 at levels 1-6). (3)+(4) **Nine cures, one routine for every temple:** Cure Blindness 1000, Cure Disease 1000, Cure Light Wounds 100, Cure Serious Wounds 350, Cure Critical Wounds 600, Neutralize Poison 1000, Raise Dead 5500, Remove Curse 3500, Stone to Flesh 2000 gold (0x4819-0x4EEB); **no Restoration, no Dispel Magic: the forum is right, temples don't cure drain;** only a Restoration scroll does (where the scrolls lie: not checked by me). **Double drain:** what the code does: each drain counts levels and HP in the record (+0x74, +0x75) and takes the level from the highest class; each Restoration gives back one level to the class whose next level costs the least and the average HP drained, so a multi-class drained twice can come back with a level in another class (Combat Chat 5's lab: an elf fighter/magic-user 3/2 drained twice came back as magic-user 3 with one level still drained). Ours does exactly this (LevelDrain, checked in camp in Combat Chat 5), so if that is the forum's "breaks", ours reproduces it; I found nothing else in the code that corrupts a record. **Combat Chat 32, the later questions:** **Bows, crossbows, slings** (ITEMS' class mask against the record's class bits, START.EXE DS:0x5D6; a multi-class may use what any of its classes may): bows 2 shots a round, fighters (and paladins) only; the light crossbow 1, fighters; the sling 1, fighters and thieves (not clerics or magic-users); darts 3, fighters, thieves, magic-users; no penalty for shooting into a melee and no friendly fire; no shot with an enemy adjacent (axes and spears strike instead); range: AC 2 better beyond a third, 5 beyond two thirds. **Stinking Cloud:** only a failed save vs poison makes a figure helpless (d4+1 rounds); a made save is "starts to cough" (effect 0x1E: AC 2 worse for a round, its casting state cleared), not helpless: the manual is wrong for the code (GAME.OVR 0x2BA88, handler 0xF6C0). Ours: helpless on a failed save as the original; on a made save ours now says STARTS TO COUGH (was IS COUGHING); 0x1E's AC and casting part is not in ours yet (unseen in a replay: Later). Lines in docs/playbook.md.

Until then: a single check-4 coins difference that passes on one re-run is reported on the board, not a block.

1. **The differential checks join the push gate** (Jeremy, Methods 5, ideas/game-modernizer.md; Lead's design): one script, `~/gbr/checks/diffcheck.sh <repo or worktree path>`, that runs all ten checks and prints per check `MATCH` or the first difference, then a last line `DIFFCHECKS 10/10` (or n/10), exit 0 only on 10/10. **Lab-independent:** the start saves, ours' start cfgs, the original's end saves and the key lists (chat 30's, with check 5's waits) copied once to `~/gbr/checks/data/` (outside git, like a lab: no game data in the repo); each run builds its own project from the given path and plays in its **own temporary copy of the game** (never a lab's slots), so main, Combat, the Player and the Lead can run it at the same time. Then add it to CLAUDE.md's "Before every push" line: **any push that touches `scripts/` or `content/` runs it; less than 10/10 blocks the push** until fixed, or the difference is explained on the board to design for Jeremy to accept. Log its wall time in docs/diff-checks.md (chat 30: about 3 minutes for all ten). **Two safeguards (design office, Lead's version):** (a) **known failures:** `~/gbr/checks/known_failures.txt`, one line per check that fails for a known reason (check, cause or "cause unknown", owning lane, date, board entry); a push may not make any check fail that isn't listed; only the owning lane adds its own line, with a board entry; the list must be empty before the acceptance run; it starts empty (chat 30: 10/10 on b242219, check 5 was chat 29's old key list). (b) **matched pairs:** a manifest per check with the hashes of its start save, its key list and the original's end save; a check whose files don't match is reported `INVALID` (counted as not matching, never as a game difference), so a stale end save (check 2, chat 26) or a stale key list (check 5, chat 29) can't pass or fail silently.

Options: (a) always on; (b) SETTINGS switch default ON; (c) switch default OFF. **Recommended: (b)** (pairs with FIX). **C11-3 Targeting skips the party (L3):** AIM's NEXT / PREV step through enemies only for attacks and harmful spells, the party first for helpful ones (CURE, BLESS); MANUAL still reaches anyone. ### Findings for everyone

- 2026-11-19 · combat (Combat Chat 28) → all · **A troll goes down in the original where ours' doesn't (✓ Combat Chat 29: a figure going down loses 0x62 and 0x3B, GAME.OVR 0x2BEF4; Rule changes).** The troll fight (regress's `orig_A_troll` save) on other seeds: 98e616ec at draw 3086 (after the facing fix) and 6444f53b at 4230: after a hit the original draws the downed troll's 3d6 (GAME.OVR 0x11434, affect 0x66: it stands up later) and ours' troll is still up, so its HP differs before (regeneration 0x62 from 0x65's start, or a revived troll's HP). The regress seed doesn't reach it. **Lead found:** on 98e616ec at draw 2972 the original's troll had 21 HP, ours 24: the original runs 0x62's +3 (affect category 0x13) only at the round's **end** (0xA087, called at 0x992B after the last turn), ours at the round's start; when 0x62 is gained (0x65, 3 rounds) is to check too. Lab README combat28, 3c. Also: **seeds as staged fights**: ours replays the original's fight from a save and a seed draw for draw, so searching seeds in ours (seconds each) for a case, then playing that seed in the original, stages a rule without hand-placing figures (combat28/bssearch2.sh).

- 2026-11-19 · combat (Combat Chat 27) → main, player, all · **Two of the original's states ours doesn't keep (both from replays; no rule change yet).** (1) **The selected member after a fight** (**corrected, Combat Chat 28: not the fight.** 0xE36E is the fight's setup freeing monsters with no room; the teardown 0x672A ends on the list's head, the first member, always. The three cases were 8:13's exit script, LOAD_CHAR 0..7 then NEW_ECL: Rule changes, 2026-11-19 combat): the teardown (GAME.OVR 0xE36E) frees each monster figure with 0x1CC10, which selects the freed figure's predecessor in the figure list (the new head when it was the head): the first member when the monsters head the list (60 of 63 fights in the Player's logs, as ours), the last member when a monster sits after the party (p22c3 fights 1 and 5, p22c4 fight 1: the next camp opened on DORN). A load ends on the first member (0x1FB99). Replays whose camp keys follow such a fight part there (p22c3 at 1184). (2) **The load word (record +0x102) is cached:** START.EXE 0x1758 (0xBA:0xBB8) recomputes it (22 callers, one at every figure's turn, 0x9A8A); 0x24CC3 / 0x24CA9 add / take coins taken or pooled; payments (training, shops, ECL) leave it, so it stays high until the next recompute (s13t's saves: ARN 1, CEDRIC 200, ELWEN 1017, LIRA 200, DORN 749 over the true sum). SHARE, TAKE and SELL test it (0x24CDD). Ours sums the true load: p21b5's 2-coin split. Lab README combat27.

What fails: begun spells (Hold Person, Prayer) go off only at the caster's next turn and a hit in melee loses them; QUICK fighters don't turn on the helpless ettins; six ettins (300 HP, two attacks each) outlast ~205 HP of party. Options: **(a)** fighters by hand too (attack the helpless and held first; tool work, an hour or two); **(b)** positioning (pull the line back into the wall's corner so fewer ettins reach; tool work, uncertain); **(c)** more levels first (main's bot's table expects level 7 here; the party is 3-6); **(d)** keep the wall's logged shortcut and take the tactical mode to the kobold king's horde (56 small figures: hand-cast Sleep and Stinking Cloud should matter much more there), then the finale. **Recommendation: (d) now, (a) as the next tactical step**; the Player goes on with (d) until answered. So fighters by hand too: each fighter attacks a helpless ettin if one is in reach, else guards. (2) **Cast before contact:** spells take effect on the caster's next turn, so cast Stinking Cloud and Prayer in round 1 from the back while the ettins close, never once they're adjacent; keep casters behind the fighters. (3) **Position:** back the party into a corner or corridor in round 1 so only two ettins can reach the front line. (4) **Kobold king:** go in fresh from the inn (as you did), and spend every magic-user's level 1 on Sleep: kobolds are 1/2 HD, Sleep takes several at once and sleeping kobolds die to any blow (point 1). (5) If still lost five more times: more levels first (the bot's party was 2-3 levels higher), then the shortcut stays. Add what works to the playbook. **Done (Player 31):** tried as advised, five more losses at each fight; more levels first asked again (Requests, "player (Player 31) → Lead").

- 2026-11-15 · main (chat 30) → design · **Poison and the full run's death rule** (for Jeremy). A member killed by poison (a wyvern's sting, a huge scorpion's: save or die) is dead with effect 0x37; in the original Slow Poison stands them up at 1 HP and a temple's NEUTRALIZE POISON cures them, which is not resurrection. Today the bot counts it as a death and reloads (Methods 4: "playthroughs never depend on resurrection"), so 8:13's wyvern (c29b) and 4:21's scorpions (c28a) end as shortcut wins though the party won those fights. Options: (a) keep it: a poison death is a death (stricter than a player would play); (b) a poisoned member counts as dead only if the party can't cure it: the bot casts Slow Poison (cleric spell 26) after the fight and takes them to a temple's NEUTRALIZE POISON on its next town visit (needs the bot to memorize Slow Poison; a small `bot.gd` change). **Recommendation: (b)**, it is how the game is played and the cure is the game's own; drained-to-death (spectres) stays a death. · **Jeremy (Q3, 19:15): (b).** The death rule's purpose: **avoid constitution loss** (Raise Dead, Resurrection); a cure without CON loss is allowed. Confirm in DOSBox that Slow Poison and NEUTRALIZE POISON leave CON unchanged. · answered · **done (chat 31)**: DOSBox (slot G's party, THRENDER dead with 0x37): Slow Poison in camp, then New Phlan's NEUTRALIZE POISON: **CON 16 before and after** (sheet and the original's own save; HP 1, OKAY, 0x37 and 0x16 gone). ### Rule changes

- 2026-11-15 · combat (Combat Chat 4) · **After a won fight the treasure menu always comes** (the original: VIEW POOL EXIT with nothing to take; TAKE only with treasure and SHARE only with money: assumed from the two seen); before, ours closed after the results when there was nothing. **The closing scene** goes by itself (three timed pages, no key). · **main's bot**: a fight with nothing to take now leaves the treasure menu open (EXIT or Esc closes it); the ending no longer waits for keys. Key lists that pressed E after such fights already match the original.
- 2026-11-15 · main (chat 25) · **The bot** (dev only, no game change): `exit <from> x,y dir <to> wall` (a step through a secret door the map draws as a wall), `warp <area> x,y x,y` (in-area teleports the path finder walks through; areas with warps have no parts), `gate ... [XXXX&M=V] <choice>` (a gate that holds only in that state), and the area search counts a way back into the start area as a new place; the plan finds 7:22's north secret door on arrival and leaves 7:23 by the rocks room. · full runs through the pyramid; reference check 87/84, 91/88 (= baseline).
- 2026-11-15 · main (chat 25) · **Loading a saved game** (original-format, and the bot's `dev_reload`): the area's init runs from the saved script image with the saved flags and **what it writes stays** (before: all flags put back after it; GAME.OVR 0x403E/0x7689); the init sees the saved square; the whole 0x1E00-byte image and the 0x6B00-0x6EFF and 0x9700-0x98FF cells are restored (before: not at all). · loads where an init resets something (7:26's 0x4A1E); scripts reading scratch cells right after a load.
- 2026-11-15 · main (chat 25) · **ECL variables are 16-bit cells** in 0x4900-0x4CFF, 0x6B00-0x6EFF, 0x9700-0x98FF (GAME.OVR 0x814A/0x82DF): a write keeps 16 bits whatever the operand type and never touches the next address; reads give the whole cell; scripts read and write the coins as words; COPY_MEM's offset and AND/OR's result are bytes. **Saves:** the original's layout (three cell arrays; byte 0x3206 the view, 2-4 in the wilderness; 0x3204-5 the wall ahead and square info), wall-set slots as the original's (wilderness: as they stand; after a load there, a new game or `LOAD_AREA_DECO 127`: wall set 0, -1, -1). · 3:0's wager, 2:9's toll, 7:26 / 8:27's tile loops; any script value above 255; ours' saves in the original (wilderness ones now load). Tyranthraxus's fight still MATCHES (1718 draws, run in ours with this change).

- 2026-11-15 · combat (Combat Chat 4) · **Tyranthraxus, from GAME.OVR, roll for roll** (check 5's fight, 1718 draws): (1) **the computer's target pick** (0x15FDF) rolls over its list up to 20 times, striking off figures it can't attack (0x13880: Invisibility 0x19 unless the attacker sees invisible 0x18, Disappearance 0x47, a vampire's mirror / holy symbol) and rolling again, first among the enemies in sight, then all; a kept target that can't be attacked is dropped (ours rolled once, and filtered the vampire's case out before the roll). (2) **The breath** (0x2A0F1): no 50% d100 until the round counter passes 0 (none in round 1), breathes on 51-100 (ours on 1-50), takes a fresh target pick, and goes as **Lightning Bolt** (0x29973): from the square beside him towards the target, a Bresenham walk of 20 half-moves that hits each new figure and bounces off walls (ours: a straight line from his own square). (3) **His free miss** (0x59, also the Cloak of Displacement's): reset by every attack in round 1, then the first attack misses once (ours: the first attack of the fight missed). (4) **Magic resistance** (0x69 / 0x6A, 0x11570): 15 (50) - 5 x (11 - spell level) in a byte, a d100 at or under it resists, first among the add-effect checks and after the elements for magic damage: Tyranthraxus resists every Pool spell, each with a draw (lab: Curse, "IS UNAFFECTED"). · the finale's fights (check 5 and the bot's 5:7); any fight with invisible or disappeared figures, vampires, a Cloak of Displacement, or Lightning-Bolt-like breath.

- 2026-11-14 · main (chat 23) · **The lab's state checks after a fight need `ptrace_scope` 0**: on Jeremy's PC (WSL) `/proc/sys/kernel/yama/ptrace_scope` is 1, so `seed.py` and `trace.py` can't read DOSBox's memory (`PermissionError` on `/proc/<pid>/mem`); `cmpfight2.sh` then shows "draws: identical" on two empty traces. Fix (resets when WSL restarts): `sudo sysctl kernel.yama.ptrace_scope=0`. Check 10 tried without it: 5 differences (clock 11:00 vs 10:57, Bakshi's HP, a dart): not meaningful, the original's fight ran on other dice.

## Findings (archived)
- 2026-11-20 · combat (Combat Chat 33) → Lead, Player, all · **The spell lab's first results** (docs/playbook.md "Spell sheet"; *seen in the original*, the computer-cast fights matched draw for draw): **Magic Missile at caster level 1 does nothing** (11 casts, no damage: the original fires level/2 missiles); **Stinking Cloud: a made save only coughs** (not helpless; kobolds resist 25%: 10 of 35 saves, all 16+); Hold Person holds kobolds 95% (cleric's 22 of 23, magic-user's 30 of 30; only a natural 20 saves); Slow has no save (9 of 9) and takes the aimed figure first; a slowed one-attack monster still strikes every other round; a wound while casting spoils the spell but the caster keeps it in memory. QUICK casters never aim an area spell whose area round an enemy in reach would catch a friend: Stinking Cloud and Sleep by hand once the lines meet, **aimed two squares off** (Sleep's 4d4 puts everything in its 3x3 to sleep, the caster too: a lone caster put himself to sleep and the party was destroyed; Fireball's area is 7x7).
